# Graph Report - ferros-grynn  (2026-10-16)

## Corpus Check
- 2835 files · ~3,719,523 words
- Verdict: corpus is large enough that graph structure adds value.

## Summary
- 61114 nodes · 163887 edges · 2034 communities (1912 shown, 122 thin omitted)
- Extraction: 97% EXTRACTED · 3% INFERRED · 0% AMBIGUOUS · INFERRED: 4140 edges (avg confidence: 0.8)
- Token cost: 0 input · 0 output

## Graph Freshness
- Built from commit: `a447d512`
- Run `git rev-parse HEAD` and compare to check if the graph is stale.
- Run `graphify update .` after code changes (no API cost).
